# SGW_Omega_Site_Dev_Master_v1

Markdown rendering of `SGW_Omega_Site_Dev_Master_v1.xlsx` from the SGW Claude Server Handoff Pack v1.2.
Generated by `xlsx_to_md.py`; cell values only (no formulas, formatting or comments).
Sheet order and headers are preserved verbatim. Evidence labels inside the
sheets control authority, per the pack's `docs/SOURCE_POLICY.md`.

## 00_README

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Workbook | SGW Omega Site — Dev Master v1 |
| Strict scope | Omega Site as the SGU hub. Local World18/80/81/82 runtime is included; Beta Site, Men'Fa, High Council, Ihpet, Hebridan, Lucia, Pen-Lai, Yotunheim, Dakara, Agnos, Tartarus, Vitrus/Anima, Ahket, Vokos Primar and Meridian are OFFWORLD pauses. Resume only at Omega handoffs/returns. |
| Resident progression paths | SGU Human, Free Jaffa and Asgard. Praxis Goa'uld/Loyalist Jaffa/OP-Core use Harset as their normal hub; cross-alliance Omega steps are retained only when raw mission text explicitly supports them. |
| Faction mission families | SGU Human: curated IDs1423-1490; Free Jaffa:1500-1519; Asgard:1522-1558 curated. These include both Omega-local and offworld field missions, with scope marked per step. |
| First arrival | SGU Human + Free Jaffa arrive via Mission1655 Shutdown. Asgard arrives via Mission1642 from High Council and joins SGU leadership. |
| Important mapping | Three missions named 'The SGU' exist: 1423/1500/1522. Dialogue sets1477/1573/1574 are respectively Human/Free-Jaffa/Asgard by strong context, but the direct MissionID↔DialogSet key is missing, so mapping is labeled inference. |
| Worlds | Omega_Site World18; Omega_Site_CmdCenter World80; Omega_Site_Ruins World81; Omega_Site_StorageRm World82. |
| Maps | Main Omega and Command Center production maps survive in SGW_Maps_006(2). Named Ruins and StorageRm production map packages were not found in the handoff. |
| Stargate | GateID5 / World18 survives with exact transform/address and full named Stargate Kismet event series. |
| Progression extent | The workbook explicitly continues beyond Yotunheim: Dakara → Omega → Agnos → Tartarus → Omega → Vitrus → Anima Vitrus → Ahket → Omega → Vokos Primar → Meridian. |
| Evidence labels | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED; USER-CONFIRMED; RECONSTRUCTION / INFERENCE; PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED / MISSING. |
| Authority rule | Raw cooked client/runtime data outranks secondary sheets. Missing grants/spawns/transition scripts are never silently invented. |

## 27_Sources

| Source | Type | Use | Caution |
|---|---|---|---|
| CookedDataMissions.jsonl | Primary raw cooked | Faction mission families, exact steps/objectives/tasks, all explicit Omega returns. | Server grant predicates and world transitions incomplete. |
| CookedDataDialogs.jsonl + CookedInteractionSet.jsonl | Primary raw cooked | Faction-specific SGU introductions, mission-giver/debrief dialogue, local Omega context. | Duplicate topic 'The SGU' requires contextual mapping. |
| WORLDS.json + STARGATES.json | Primary raw cooked | World18/80/81/82 and Gate5. | World81/82 map packages missing from file index. |
| CookedDataKismet*.jsonl | Primary raw cooked | Omega Stargate + multiple Asgard teleporter sequences, including Command Center. | Event semantics not fully decoded. |
| CookedDataItems.jsonl / Effects | Primary raw cooked | Sensors, Space Matter Compressor, O'Neill files, letter, bomber/Runk'an mechanics. | Exact task binding sometimes missing. |
| FULL_CLIENT_FILE_INDEX.jsonl | File audit | 134 main Omega + 26 Command Center files; missing named Ruins/StorageRm. | Current handoff may be incomplete. |
| Planets & Locations.xlsx | Secondary project source | Omega landmark coordinates and subworld entrances. | Secondary coordinates; cooked gate/raw map higher authority. |
| SGW_ Progression.xlsx | Progression aid | All three SGU paths and repeated Omega returns through Meridian. | Use for route/hub markers, not invented missions. |
| SGW_ Milky Way Missions_Dev_Ver.xlsx | Secondary mission workbook | Omega sheet is effectively empty. | Raw cooked data therefore drives this reconstruction. |
